## 2026-10-12 T-V1-008 (Ask helper passes an object, not a string)
TASK: T-V1-008 (MICRO-TICKETS-V1, trace RP-06 / QF-06)
CHANGE:
  - apps/web/src/app/ask/contracts.ts — typed `AskRequest` + `buildAskBody(query, model?)`, `askQuestion` now passes that OBJECT to `auth.authFetch` (no `JSON.stringify`, no manual Content-Type header); doc comment marks auth-client as the single serialization boundary.
  - apps/web/src/app/ask/contracts.test.ts — NEW (5 tests): body `typeof === "object"` and not a String instance, path/method shape, model-omitted case, error propagation preserved.
WHY: spec instruction "pass the typed request object to authFetch; delete the JSON.stringify at the helper" — ends double serialization on Ask (QF-06). NOTE: the raw `JSON.stringify` + `headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"}` removal had ALREADY landed in HEAD commit 7c452f3 (coordinator QF-06 fix); this ticket adds the typed body + the missing unit test on top. No string-sniffing heuristic added (reviewer checklist #1).
REUSED: none (no new deps); mirrors the existing pure-helper test pattern of apps/web/src/app/search/contracts.test.ts (T-V1-009 target) and existing vitest/node config in apps/web/vitest.config.ts.
BUILT: `AskRequest`/`buildAskBody` — small custom glue because ask/contracts.ts had an inline untyped body literal; extraction makes the emitted body directly assertable. Reuse of a shared type did not fit: authFetch's `init.body` is intentionally `unknown`.
VERIFY:
  - cd apps/web && npx vitest run  → 6 files / 55 tests passed (incl. src/app/ask/contracts.test.ts 5 passed)
  - cd apps/web && npx tsc --noEmit → clean (no output)
  - backend model accepts: /root/ko-venv/bin/python -c "…AskRequest.model_validate({'query':'what is RP-06?','model':'gpt-x'})" → parsed AskRequest {'query': 'what is RP-06?', 'model': 'gpt-x', 'mode': <RetrievalMode.HYBRID: 'hybrid'>}; no-model variant {'query':'plain query','model':None,…} also parses. (Success criterion: helper emits object, real AskRequest parses it — met.)
RISKS: low — behavior at the wire is identical (auth-client.ts:293 still does the one `JSON.stringify`); retry/401-refresh path in authFetch untouched (test asserts failures still propagate). Runtime e2e through the real proxy is NOT proven here — that is T-V1-012/T-V1-011's job. No deploy/restart/commit performed. Foreign changes observed in worktree during this ticket (dev1 backend + reviewers, NOT touched by me): packages/answer/models.py, packages/retrieval/adapters/pg_fts.py, packages/retrieval/adapters/qdrant_store.py, packages/core/errors.py (new), tests/unit/test_answer_engine.py, tests/unit/test_error_taxonomy.py, tests/unit/test_no_false_success.py, various handoffs/*.md.

## 2026-10-12 T-V1-010 (One documented serialization boundary in auth-client)
TASK: T-V1-010 (MICRO-TICKETS-V1 §B, trace RP-06/RP-07 · QF-06/QF-07)
CHANGE:
  - apps/web/src/lib/auth-client.ts — new module-level `serializeBody(body, headers)` + `isWireReadyBody` + case-insensitive `hasHeader`. Rules: (1) `undefined` → no body, no header; (2) wire-ready BodyInit — string, FormData/File, Blob, ArrayBuffer, ArrayBufferView (TypedArray/DataView), URLSearchParams, ReadableStream — forwarded BY REFERENCE, untouched, with NO content-type added by us (a hand-set content-type would drop FormData's runtime `boundary=…`); (3) everything else (objects/arrays/numbers/booleans/null) → `JSON.stringify(body)` ONCE + `content-type: application/json` only when the caller left it unset. `authFetch.call()` now calls it (GET still sends no body); `authPost` (login/register/logout) re-routes through the SAME function, so request-body `JSON.stringify` in this file went 2 → 1 — only serializeBody (line 185); the other remaining `JSON.stringify` (line 228) is safeFetch's synthetic 502 RESPONSE literal. Boundary documented in: a ~40-line contract block above `serializeBody`, a file-header bullet, an `AuthFetchInit.body` doc comment, and a pointer on `authFetch`.
  - apps/web/src/lib/auth-client.test.ts — +9 contract tests in `describe("serialization boundary (T-V1-010)")` (22 → 32): object-in/object-out encoded exactly once (`JSON.parse(body)` deep-equals input AND is not a string); FormData same-instance passthrough + parts intact + no content-type; ArrayBuffer/TypedArray identity + byte equality + no content-type; NO string sniffing (pre-JSON string `'{"already":"json","n":1}'` byte-identical, `'plain text'` not re-quoted, no content-type guessed); JSON-looking string nested in an object stays data; caller-supplied `Content-Type: application/merge-patch+json` respected; GET never carries a body; FormData re-sent as the SAME instance across 401 → refresh → retry (1 refresh, 2 attempts); AuthApiError still thrown on a body-carrying POST; login goes through serializeBody (once).
WHY: ticket instruction "JSON-serialize plain objects exactly once; pass FormData/binary through untouched; document the boundary" — metrics: serialization decision points N → 1, heuristic branches: 0 (dispatch is on the runtime TYPE of the body, never on the CONTENT of a value; no "does this string already look like JSON?" branch exists anywhere). Satisfies RP-06/QF-06 (single boundary that T-V1-008/T-V1-009 hand objects to) and keeps the RP-07/QF-07 binary path explicit for T-V1-012/T-V1-013.
REUSED: none (no new deps). Reused this file's existing injectable-fetcher harness (`scriptedFetcher`/`jsonResponse`/`createAuthClient`) and the existing vitest node config at apps/web/vitest.config.ts.
BUILT: `serializeBody`/`isWireReadyBody`/`hasHeader` — ~20 lines of custom type-dispatch glue, because `AuthFetchInit.body` is intentionally `unknown` and must branch on wire-readiness instead of guessing. Reusing a serializer lib did not fit: adding a dependency for one `JSON.stringify` would move the boundary, not collapse it.
VERIFY:
  - cd apps/web && npx vitest run → 6 files / 70 tests passed (auth-client 32, ask 5, search 15, trace-panel 8, status-chip 4, backend proxy route 6)
  - cd apps/web && npx tsc --noEmit → exit 0 (no output)
  - cd apps/web && npx eslint src/lib/auth-client.ts src/lib/auth-client.test.ts → exit 0
  - T-008/T-009 helpers unaffected: ask/contracts.test.ts 5 passed + search/contracts.test.ts 15 passed in the run above; plus an integration probe living OUTSIDE the repo tree (/tmp/opencode/tv1-010-probe, 2 passed) that drives the REAL singleton auth client with the REAL helpers: askQuestion → `POST /api/backend/v1/workspaces/ws-1/ask`, `JSON.parse(body)` === `buildAskBody("what is RP-06?","gpt-x")` and is not a string, content-type application/json; searchDocuments → same for `/search` with `buildSearchBody(...)`. Re-run: `cd /tmp/opencode/tv1-010-probe && "/root/code/Super Rag/knowledge-os/apps/web/node_modules/.bin/vitest" run --config ./vitest.config.mjs`
  - no sniffing / one decision: `grep -n "JSON.stringify" apps/web/src/lib/auth-client.ts` → line 185 (serializeBody) + line 228 (safeFetch synthetic 502 response literal) only; `JSON.parse` appears once, at `readJson` (RESPONSE side).
RISKS: low — wire behavior for every current caller is byte-identical (all `body:` call sites pass object literals or undefined; full suite + tsc + eslint green). Two deliberate, documented deltas: (a) a caller-supplied `content-type` is now respected instead of being clobbered by `application/json`; (b) a raw STRING body passes through verbatim instead of being quoted into JSON — no current caller passes a string, and the alternative requires exactly the string-sniffing this ticket forbids. FormData/Blob now reach the proxy untouched, but the Next proxy (`apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/backend/[...path]/route.ts`) is still text/arrayBuffer-based — byte-identity end-to-end is T-V1-012/T-V1-013, browser e2e is T-V1-011. A one-shot `ReadableStream` body cannot be replayed across the 401 retry (documented in the contract block). No commit/deploy/restart performed. Allowed files honored: `apps/web/src/lib/auth-client.ts` + `apps/web/src/lib/auth-client.test.ts` + this handoff row; the probe lives in /tmp/opencode (outside the repo). FOREIGN paths observed during this ticket and NOT touched by me (parallel dev1/reviewers): `M` apps/api/app/authz/principal.py, apps/api/app/documents/reads.py, apps/api/app/documents/upload.py, apps/worker/jobs/ingest_worker.py, packages/answer/engine.py, packages/graph/extractor.py, tests/integration/test_ingestion_jobs.py; `??` packages/core/errors.py, tests/contracts/test_error_taxonomy_contract.py, tests/unit/test_error_taxonomy.py, handoffs/CODE1-Q0-HANDOFF.md (plus the handoffs/*.md dirt already present when I started).

## 2026-10-12 T-V1-012 (Next proxy forwards raw/streaming upload bodies)

TASK: T-V1-012 (MICRO-TICKETS-V1 §B, trace RP-07 / QF-07; shares the T-V1-010 transport contract — no JSON re-serialization)
CHANGE:
  - apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/backend/[...path]/route.ts — request bodies now cross the proxy as RAW BYTES on a stream:
    - removed the body materialization (`request.text()` for everything except multipart/octet-stream, `request.arrayBuffer()` for those two) — the UTF-8 decode→re-encode was the byte-mutation bug (RP-07);
    - new `MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 25 MiB` (mirrors `packages/ingestion/normalize.py` `MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES`), exported; new exported `UploadTooLargeError` + `boundedBodyStream(source, limit)` — a TransformStream passthrough that enqueues chunks UNCHANGED while counting them; overflow does `controller.error(typed)` (never buffers past the cap, aborts the in-flight upstream upload); cancel propagates bounded→source (defined cancellation both ways);
    - advisory `Content-Length` fence (isdigit && > cap → 413 `upload_too_large`, mirroring `apps/api/app/documents/upload.py`) BEFORE any byte is read or the backend is contacted; actual-count enforcement happens mid-stream for absent/lying lengths;
    - `content-type` forwarded VERBATIM incl. multipart `boundary=…` (comment added);
    - fetch init: `{ body: boundedStream, duplex: "half" }` (typed `RequestInit & { duplex: "half" }` — DOM lib lacks `duplex`) + `AbortSignal.any([AbortSignal.timeout(15_000), request.signal])`;
    - catch: stream teardown `body.cancel()` (no dangling upload), `instanceof UploadTooLargeError` → 413, client hang-up (`request.signal.aborted`) → 499 `client_disconnected`, else the EXISTING 502 timeout/unreachable shape+messages unchanged.
  - apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/backend/[...path]/route.test.ts — +8 tests (6 → 14): 3-stage byte identity on a hostile invalid-UTF-8 multipart fixture (arrival-time first-chunk = fixture prefix; boundary verbatim), non-multipart octet-stream byte identity through the real `POST` handler, JSON byte-for-byte (parses to the same object at the handler — no re-serialization), Content-Length fence → 413 pre-contact (fetch never called), mid-stream overflow → 413 (exactly 24 MiB forwarded, 7th chunk cut, 0 bytes stored, infinite source torn down), client cancel → 499 + upstream aborted + inbound source cancelled, `boundedBodyStream` verbatim-passthrough + typed overflow error, `boundedBodyStream` cancel→source propagation.
WHY: spec instructions (1) "replace request.text() re-encoding with bounded raw/stream body forwarding", (2) "preserve multipart content-type + boundary; enforce size limit + cancellation". Metric "byte-mutation … → identical (after)": hostile fixture is 189 B raw and the OLD path renders it 205 B (+16, mutated — see control below); AFTER: 189/189 identical at all 3 stages. T-V1-010 contract honored: the proxy neither parses nor stringifies — the bytes auth-client's single `JSON.stringify` produced reach FastAPI untouched (serialization decisions stay at 1, heuristic branches 0).
REUSED: none (no new deps). Node/web-platform primitives only: `TransformStream`/`ReadableStream` passthrough, `AbortSignal.any` (env Node v22.22.1), existing vitest node config, existing `forward()` + stubbed-fetch harness and fixtures in route.test.ts.
BUILT: `boundedBodyStream` + `UploadTooLargeError` + `uploadTooLargeResponse` (~40 lines custom glue) — the bound must hold on the ACTUAL byte count while streaming: a pre-buffer would reintroduce unbounded memory, and a content-length-only check trusts the client. An upload-size library would wrap the same TransformStream, not remove it.

    AT-HANDLER      bytes=189  sha256=a8f215055fcb5cf2ed5b805dc1b402912fc9e1e485228fa2aa23b87bf37b719d  (first chunk bytes=189 sha256=a8f215055fcb5cf2ed5b805dc1b402912fc9e1e485228fa2aa23b87bf37b719d)

    JSON AT-HANDLER  bytes=57  sha256=5729e99baa6beab3b199d781b55105fa89bd46d67fecae631e5ac10a36d540c8   (equal; JSON.parse round-trips to the same object)

## 2026-10-12 T-V1-013 (Upload byte-identity proof — hostile invalid-UTF-8 multipart fixture)

TASK: T-V1-013 (MICRO-TICKETS-V1 §B, trace RP-07 / QF-07; depends T-012; dispatch layer = frontend/API-proxy e2e beside `route.test.ts`)
CHANGE:  - NEW `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/backend/[...path]/hostile-upload.fixture.ts` — byte-EXACT hostile multipart/form-data fixture (body built from explicit byte arrays, so no editor/git filter can "repair" it): boundary `----kios-tv1013-boundary`, 3 text fields (`title` non-ASCII UTF-8 / `collection` / `workspace`) + a `file` part (`filename="hostile-µ-ticket.bin"`, `application/octet-stream`) whose 630-byte payload carries NULs, invalid leads FF/FE, lone continuations 80–BF, overlongs (C0 AF, E0 80 AF, C1 BF), surrogate-half ED A0 80, beyond-U+10FFFF leads (F5/F8), a TRUNCATED sequence as its final bytes, RFC-legal boundary look-alikes (`--KIOS-TV1013-BOUNDARY--`, `--kios-tv1013-bound` — the boundary VALUE never appears in content), 512 deterministic LCG bytes; plus `fixtureChunks()` (uneven cuts: mid-file-header, first payload byte, mid-payload, past payload), `sha256()`, `fingerprint()`.
  - NEW `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/backend/[...path]/route.upload-byte-identity.test.ts` — 7 tests driving the REAL `forward()` from `route.ts` (only global `fetch` + `next/headers` stubbed, same harness as `route.test.ts`): (1) hostility control — a `text()`-style UTF-8 round-trip changes both length and SHA, invalid bytes present, boundary absent from content, fixture reassembles exactly from its chunks; (2) **3-stage identity** — pre-transport == at-handler == stored on len + SHA-256 (3/3 stages, one SHA / one length), first chunk off the wire is an exact prefix of the fixture, forwarded `content-type` verbatim incl. `boundary=`; (3) **multipart fields intact** — a delimiter-accurate test-side parser re-parses the body at handler AND stored: names `[title,collection,workspace,file]`, exact field values, filename + part content-type, file-part bytes SHA == fixture file bytes, payload still invalid UTF-8 (no U+FFFD "repair"); (4) oversize declared `Content-Length` → clean 413 `upload_too_large`, backend never contacted, 0 bytes moved, 0 partial files; (5) oversize ACTUAL count (absent/lying length, 4 MiB hostile-fixture chunks, infinite source) → clean 413, exactly 24 MiB crossed, **0 bytes persisted (no partial file)**, source torn down (<15 pulls); (6) client cancel → 499 `client_disconnected`, upstream aborted, inbound body cancelled, bytes had flowed but **0 persisted (no partial file)**; (7) ordinary JSON upload path byte-identical + parses to the same object (success criteria "JSON path still green").
WHY: spec instruction "length + SHA equal pre-transport / at-handler / stored; fields intact; oversize → clean reject, no partial; cancel → no partial" — the T-V1-012 fix shipped the behavior, this ticket pins it with proof. Metric "SHA match 3/3 stages" is asserted as `new Set(stage.sha256).size === 1` over the three fingerprints; metric "partial-file leaks: 0" is asserted as `store.partialFiles() === 0` in BOTH oversize paths and the cancel path, via a store that only `commit()`s when the handler read the body to completion (so "no partial" is a real assertion, not a tautology: test 5 records 24 MiB in flight, test 6 records >0 in flight, and both still show 0 committed).
REUSED: none (no new deps). Node/web-platform primitives only — `TransformStream`/`ReadableStream`, `node:crypto`, the existing vitest node config, and the `forward()` + stubbed-fetch harness pattern already in `route.test.ts`.
BUILT: the fixture builder + a ~40-line delimiter-accurate multipart parser (test-side). Reuse did not fit: no multipart parser ships in this package, and the parser must be *hostile-tolerant* (it scans for `\r\n--boundary` followed by CRLF or `--` and skips look-alikes, where a naive `split(boundary)` mis-parses) — a generic parser would hide exactly the framing corruption this test exists to catch. The fixture is a byte-array builder rather than a stored `.bin` so the hostile bytes cannot be normalized by tooling.
VERIFY (commands + output, verbatim):
  - `cd apps/web && npx vitest run "src/app/(auth)/api/backend/[...path]/route.upload-byte-identity.test.ts"` → `Test Files 1 passed (1) / Tests 7 passed (7)` (run 3× → 7/7 each time, no flakes).
  - `cd apps/web && npx vitest run` → `Test Files 7 passed (7) / Tests 85 passed (85)` = 78 pre-existing (incl. T-V1-012 route.test 14, T-V1-010 auth-client 32, T-V1-008 ask 5, T-V1-009 search 15) + 7 new — ordinary JSON path green.
  - `cd apps/web && npx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0. `npx eslint` on both new files → exit 0.
  - MUTATION CHECK (does the proof actually bite? throwaway script `/tmp/opencode/tv1013-mutation.sh`, OUTSIDE the repo; `route.ts` backed up, mutated, restored — post-restore `git diff --stat -- route.ts` printed NOTHING, file byte-identical, HEAD unchanged): three regressions injected at once — M1 byte mutation on the forward path (`0xff→0x2a` in `boundedBodyStream`), M2 mid-stream overflow no longer mapped to a clean 413, M3 client-cancel 499 branch disabled → `Tests 4 failed | 3 passed (7)`: identity ✗, fields ✗, mid-stream oversize ✗ (got 502), cancel ✗ (got 502). Passing 3 = the control + declared-413 fence + JSON test, i.e. exactly the paths the mutations don't touch. No mutation needed for "no partial": with the bound removed the oversize test would never terminate (infinite source), which the `<15 pulls` + timeout assertions catch.
METRICS: SHA match across stages: 3/3 (one SHA, one length). Partial-file leaks: 0 (3 assert sites: declared-oversize, actual-oversize, cancel). Multipart fields verified intact: 4/4 parts, parsed at 2 stages. Serialization decision points on this path: still 1 (T-V1-010 contract untouched — this ticket added zero code to `route.ts`).
RISKS: low — zero product-file changes (test + fixture only); full suite 85/85, tsc + eslint clean, 3/3 repeat runs stable. Evidence-layer caveat for the reviewer: this is a proxy-layer e2e with the network stubbed (the ticket's dispatch layer), so it proves the shipped `forward()` byte path, not the real FastAPI side — the backend's own `_read_limited`/store commit is modeled by the test's commit-on-complete store. ## 2026-10-12 T-V1-041 (Proxy timeout covers slow Asks — 15s → 30s documented cap)

TASK: T-V1-041 (MICRO-TICKETS-V1, trace T-V1-011 finding: `APP_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=15s` vs 8.6–16.7s measured gateway Ask latency)
CHANGE:
  - apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/backend/[...path]/route.ts —
    - `APP_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS` raised `15_000 → 30_000` and now EXPORTED, with a doc comment that documents the cap in-place: what it bounds (the WHOLE upstream exchange — headers AND body — because the composed signal stays attached to the body stream), why 30 s (T-V1-011 measured 8.6–16.7 s for a GOOD Ask; at 15 s the proxy turned those 200s into "backend_unreachable" 502s; 30 s ≈ 2x margin over the ~17 s worst case), and that it is a fixed hard deadline (never unbounded; client hang-up can only end it earlier).
    - new `upstreamFailureResponse(error, base, request, deadlineFired)` helper — single mapping of a failed upstream exchange to the error contract (client-hung-up → 499 `client_disconnected`; deadline/`TimeoutError` → 502 `backend_unreachable` "timed out"; other → 502 "unreachable"). Behavior of the pre-headers path is byte-for-byte the same statuses/codes as before (the inline 499/502 literals moved into the helper).
    - `upstream.text()` (the BODY read) now sits in a try/catch that maps failures through the same helper: previously a body that stalled/errored AFTER headers escaped `forward()` as an unhandled rejection → opaque 500 from the route handler; now it reports the documented 499/502 contract. Fast path (a body that arrives) untouched. Signal plumbing: the composed signal is kept in `upstreamSignal` so the body-failure branch can tell "deadline fired" from "client hung up".
  - NEW apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/backend/[...path]/route.timeout.test.ts — 5 tests, same stubbed-fetch/`next/headers` harness as route.test.ts driving the REAL `forward()`/`POST()`: (1) cap documented + bounded — finite, `> 16_700` (measured worst case), `<= 60_000` (no unbounded wait); (2) **headline: stub 16 s backend → 200, not 502** — the stub resolves after 16 s OR rejects with the abort error `AbortSignal.timeout` produces (`DOMException "TimeoutError"`) the moment the proxy's signal fires, exactly like undici; asserts status 200 + body + `elapsed >= 16_000` + upstream signal never aborted (real 16 s wait — the wait IS the evidence; per-test timeout raised to 26 s); (3) fast paths unchanged — composed `AbortSignal` attached but unfired, auth + content-type forwarded verbatim; (4) cancel propagates — client hang-up → 499 `client_disconnected` AND the upstream request's signal aborted; (5) post-headers body stall + client hang-up → clean 499 through the new guard (would be an unhandled rejection before).
WHY: spec instruction "raise/align the deadline (or stream-aware) so a 200-answer is never converted to 502; add slow-upstream test (stub 16s backend → 200 not 502)". Raised fixed cap chosen over stream-aware deadline because the Ask path is plain JSON (backend computes, then responds — headers and body arrive together; there is no SSE chunking at this proxy to be deadline-per-chunk about); 30 s satisfies reviewer checklist #1 (cap documented IN the route, finite, asserted `<= 60 s`) and costs nothing on fast paths (#3 — ceiling not delay; all 85 pre-existing tests green) while #2 (cancel propagates) is preserved by the untouched `AbortSignal.any([timeout, request.signal])` composition plus new tests 4/5.
REUSED: none (no new deps). Existing primitives only: `AbortSignal.any`/`AbortSignal.timeout` (already in the file), the route.test.ts stubbed-fetch harness, existing vitest node config.
BUILT: `upstreamFailureResponse` (~30 lines) — small custom glue because the same error contract must be reachable from two failure points (pre-headers `fetch` rejection, post-headers body failure) and duplicating the 499/502 literals would let the two surfaces drift. No library fits (this is the proxy's own wire contract). Note: `AbortSignal.timeout` is NOT interceptable by vitest fake timers (native timer, unref'd), so the slow-upstream test waits the real 16 s rather than faking it — a fake-clock version would prove nothing about the real deadline.
VERIFY (commands + output, verbatim):
  - `cd apps/web && npx vitest run "src/app/(auth)/api/backend/[...path]/route.timeout.test.ts"` → `Test Files 1 passed (1) / Tests 5 passed (5)` — the 16 s test measured `16031ms` (real wait, not a shortcut).
  - `cd apps/web && npx vitest run` (post-restore final state) → `Test Files 8 passed (8) / Tests 90 passed (90)` = 85 pre-existing (route.test 14, upload-byte-identity 7, auth-client 32, ask 5, search 15, trace-panel 8, status-chip 4) + 5 new — **fast paths unchanged**.
  - `cd apps/web && npx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0 (clean). `npx eslint route.ts route.timeout.test.ts` → exit 0 (clean).
  - MUTATION CHECK (does the proof bite? backup in /tmp/opencode OUTSIDE the repo; `route.ts` mutated `30_000 → 15_000`, run, then restored from backup — post-restore `cmp route.ts /tmp/opencode/tv1041-route.ts.bak` → byte-identical, `grep` confirms `30_000`): → `Tests 2 failed | 3 passed (5)` — failing exactly on the bug class: headline test `→ expected 502 to be 200` (the 16 s upstream got converted to 502 under the old cap — the literal regression this ticket closes) and the cap-doc test `→ expected 15000 to be greater than 16700`. The 3 that stayed green = fast path, cancel propagation, body-stall guard — i.e. exactly the paths independent of the cap value. Re-run after restore: 5/5 green (3 green runs of the slow test total: initial, full-suite ×2).
METRICS: 16 s-upstream cases converted to 502: 0 (test 2 asserts 200; mutation proves it fails when the cap is 15 s). New proxy-timeout tests: 5 → green. Cap: finite, documented in-route, asserted `<= 60_000` (no unbounded wait).
RISKS: low — full suite 90/90, tsc + eslint clean, 3/3 green repeat runs. Deliberate deltas for the reviewer: (1) the suite now takes ~18 s wall (was <1 s) — the extra ~16 s is exactly the slow-upstream evidence, isolated in one test with an explicit 26 s per-test timeout; if the coordinator wants it out of the default gate, it can be tagged/skipped in CI, but the spec asks for this test green. (2) `upstream.text()` failures now answer the documented 499/502 contract instead of an unhandled 500 — an error-path-only change (no fast path touched); pre-headers statuses/codes byte-identical. (3) 30 s is a proxy-layer cap only: a future streaming/SSE Ask (per-chunk heartbeats) would want a stream-aware (idle-gap) deadline instead — out of scope here (Ask is plain JSON today; the backend computes then responds). (4) The `UploadTooLargeError`-identity-across-fetch-boundary caveat (T-V1-012) stands unchanged. No commit / no deploy / no restart. Allowed files honored: `route.ts` + NEW `route.timeout.test.ts` + this handoff row; `route.test.ts`, the T-V1-013 fixture/test and every other path untouched (`route.ts` was temporarily mutated for the mutation check from a /tmp backup and restored byte-identical — see VERIFY).

## 2026-10-12 T-V1-032 (Per-message citations + authorized passage fetch with span highlight)

TASK: T-V1-032 (MICRO-TICKETS-V1, trace RP-13 / QF-13; depends T-008–010 transport, T-022 canonical source)
CHANGE:
  - apps/web/src/app/ask/components/chat-window.tsx — new exported `MessageCitation` (= frozen `Citation` + optional `content`/`bbox`/`grounding_score`, declared HERE instead of editing `ask/contracts.ts` so the allowed-file list stays honored) and `ChatMessage` (role, content, claims, citations, status, reason_code, reason, confidence, limitations, error) + builders `userMessage` / `answerMessage` / `failureMessage` + resolver `citationForChunk(message, chunkId)`. `ChatWindow` now takes `messages` ONLY — its `citations` and `claimCitations` props are DELETED (that was the shared/latest-answer lookup), claim chips resolve against `msg.citations` via `citationForChunk`, and a new `MessageMeta` block renders that message's own status badge, confidence (level/basis/probability), reason and limitations list.
  - apps/web/src/app/ask/components/source-panel.tsx — passage fetch + highlight: exports `buildPassageUrl`, `requestPassage`, `resolvePassage`, `segmentPassage` (pure, testable) and the `PassageView` union (`loading | ready(origin canonical|answer_embedded, notice) | error | empty`). Panel takes `citation` + `workspaceId`, fetches `/api/passage?workspace_id=&chunk_id=` on the clicked citation, renders provenance (document/page/section/span/bbox) + passage with `<mark>` around the cited char span, an origin badge and the failure `notice`.
  - apps/web/src/app/ask/page.tsx — DELETED the `answer` state and the `citations` / `claimCitations` derivations; transcript is `ChatMessage[]` built by the builders above; `selectedSource` holds the clicked citation itself and passes `workspaceId` to the panel.
  - NEW apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/passage/route.ts — THE one API passage-fetch route: `GET /api/passage?workspace_id=…&chunk_id=…` → server-side `kios_access` cookie → `Authorization: Bearer` → backend canonical read `GET /v1/workspaces/{ws}/chunks/{chunk_id}`, normalized `{ok:true, passage:{chunk_id, content, document_id, filename, page, section, span, bbox, workspace_id}}`, error taxonomy `invalid_request 400 / unauthorized 401 / forbidden 403 / passage_unavailable 404 / client_disconnected 499 / backend_unreachable 502 / passage_malformed 502`.
  - NEW tests: apps/web/src/app/ask/citations.test.ts (9), apps/web/src/app/ask/passage.test.ts (18), apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/passage/route.test.ts (18) = +45 tests.
  - handoffs/CODE2-HANDOFF.md — this row.
WHY: spec instructions 1–2. (1) "Store citations + confidence + limitations + status per message (not off the latest answer)" — the page previously held a single `answer` state and ChatWindow a module-shared `citations` prop, so message N's chips re-resolved against whatever answer was latest (the exact mixup class RP-13/QF-13 target). Citations are now frozen ON the message by `answerMessage` (arrays COPIED, not aliased) and every lookup is `citationForChunk(msg, …)`, which returns null instead of searching elsewhere. (2) "authorized canonical passage + span/page/bbox highlight on citation click" — a dedicated route is the seam that keeps ACL server-side (R-004: the browser never reads a chunk directly, the backend authorizes the bearer for that workspace) and owns the honest error taxonomy, rather than reusing the generic proxy (which would forward an unvalidated path and a raw upstream body). Deps T-V1-022: the canonical-source read does not exist yet, so upstream 404 → explicit `passage_unavailable` (message names T-V1-022), rendered as a labeled notice — never a blank panel and never another message's source.
REUSED: none (no new deps, no package.json change). Reused existing seams: `next/headers` `cookies()` + `ACCESS_COOKIE` and the `getBackendBaseUrl()` helper (same pattern as `app/(auth)/api/backend/[...path]/route.ts`), `AbortSignal.any`/`AbortSignal.timeout` (already used by that proxy), the vitest node config + the `vi.mock("next/headers")` / stubbed-`fetch` harness of `route.test.ts`, and the pure-helper-out-of-a-`.tsx`-component test pattern of `search/components/trace-panel.test.ts`.
BUILT: the passage route (~230 lines incl. `readPassageParams`/`buildPassageUpstreamUrl`/`normalizePassage`/`GET`) and the panel's `requestPassage`/`resolvePassage`/`segmentPassage` — custom glue because no library owns (a) cookie→bearer exchange for one backend read, (b) normalization of an upstream body into the single `passage` shape with honest failure codes, or (c) clamped char-span segmentation for `<mark>` highlighting. Reusing a generic HTTP client would have moved the ACL decision into the browser (forbidden by R-004) instead of collapsing it into one server-side call.
VERIFY (commands + output):
  - `cd apps/web && npx vitest run` → `Test Files 11 passed (11) / Tests 135 passed (135)` (90 pre-existing + 45 new; new = ask/citations 9, ask/passage 18, passage route 18).
  - `cd apps/web && npx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0. `npx eslint` (whole app) → exit 0. `npx eslint src/app/ask "src/app/(auth)/api/passage"` → exit 0.
  - MUTATION CHECK #1 (does the independence test bite? `chat-window.tsx` backed up to /tmp/opencode OUTSIDE the repo, mutated to resolve citations through a module-level "latest built answer" registry — i.e. the original bug — run, then restored; `cmp` → byte-identical): `Tests 5 failed | 4 passed (9)`, failures exactly on the cross-message assertions (`citationForChunk(messages[0], "chunk-b")` → beta.pdf instead of null; duplicate-chunk-id case `expected 'other.pdf' to be 'same.pdf'`). Restored → 9/9 green.
  - MUTATION CHECK #2 (does the explicit-404 test bite? `route.ts` backed up, upstream-404 mapping `404 passage_unavailable → 500 backend_unreachable`, run, restored, `cmp` byte-identical): `Tests 1 failed | 17 passed (18)` — `expected 500 to be 404`. Restored → 18/18 green, full suite 135/135 re-run after both restores.
  - Not run (deliberately): `next build` — a `next-server` process and the compose stack are live (docker proxies :3001/:8000) and writing `.next` would touch a running service; no runtime/browser e2e was performed (that is T-V1-011's scope).
METRICS: cross-message citation mixups: 0 (mixup test + mutation proof above: a shared/latest registry fails 5/9). Click-to-passage works: yes at the seam (route 200 → `passage` → panel `ready` + span `<mark>`, unit-proven end-to-end through `requestPassage`→`resolvePassage`→`segmentPassage`); the CANONICAL leg is blocked on T-V1-022 and is reported truthfully as `passage_unavailable` + an `answer-embedded` badge until it lands. Loading/error/empty states: all reachable and tested (9 assertions).
REVIEWER CHECKLIST: (1) no shared citation list — `page.tsx` has no `answer` state, `ChatWindow` no `citations`/`claimCitations` prop, every chip resolves via `citationForChunk(msg, …)`; (2) passage canonical + ACL-checked — server-side bearer forwarded to the backend canonical read, `readPassageParams` blocks separators/traversal, response `no-store`, and the panel DISCLOSES provenance (`Canonical (live)` vs `Answer-embedded` + notice); (3) loading/error/empty states — all three explicit, `role="status"`/`role="alert"`, tested.
RISKS: medium-low. (a) The canonical leg 404s until T-V1-022 lands — handled explicitly, but until then the panel shows the citation's OWN answer-embedded `content` (text the backend hydrated for THIS citation in THIS message, ACL-checked at Ask time) with an `Answer-embedded` badge and the upstream `code: message` notice. Reviewer should confirm they accept that labeled fallback; the strict alternative (error state only) would make click-to-passage show nothing until T-V1-022. (b) `content`/`bbox` are NOT on the frozen `ask/contracts.ts` `Citation` type — read via the locally declared `MessageCitation` extension; if the backend ever stops sending `content`, (a) degrades to the explicit error state (no crash). (c) `/api/passage` uses plain same-origin `fetch`, NOT `authFetch`, so it does not participate in the 401→refresh→retry flow: an expired session yields an explicit `unauthorized` notice (or the embedded fallback), not a redirect to login. (d) No browser/runtime verification in this ticket (no build, no restart, no deploy, no commit). Allowed files honored: only `ask/components/{chat-window,source-panel}.tsx`, `ask/page.tsx`, ONE new route + its test, 2 new test files and this row.

## 2026-10-12 T-V1-033 (History persists, scoped to account/workspace; honest in-flight claim; explicit deleted source)

TASK: T-V1-033 (MICRO-TICKETS-V1, trace RP-13 / QF-13; depends T-032; estimate M)
CHANGE:
  - NEW `apps/web/src/app/ask/history.ts` — THE persistence layer. `HistoryScope = {account, workspace}`; key `kios_ask_history:v1:{account}:{workspace}` behind a charset gate (`/^[A-Za-z0-9._@+-]{1,128}$/` on BOTH parts, else `historyStorageKey` returns `null` → nothing is persisted, so a `:`-bearing scope can never make two pairs collide on one key); payload is a versioned envelope `{version, account, workspace, saved_at, messages}` that REPEATS the scope (defence in depth: key + payload must agree or the read yields `[]`); `loadHistory` / `saveHistory` / `clearHistory` / `readEnvelope` / `normalizeMessage` / `browserStorage` exports; bounded to `MAX_PERSISTED_MESSAGES = 200`; every failure path returns "empty / not persisted" instead of throwing (missing store, private-mode `SecurityError`, `QuotaExceededError`, corrupt JSON, unknown `version`, malformed message → dropped individually, wrong-typed optional fields dropped, unknown `status` dropped).
  - NEW `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/account-scope/route.ts` — `GET /api/account-scope` → `200 {ok:true, scope}` with `cache-control: no-store`. Server reads the httpOnly `kios_access` cookie and returns its `sub` as the ACCOUNT half of the key; `decodeAccountScope` requires a 3-segment token, decodable JSON, a string `sub` ≤128 chars that passes the key charset, and `type` either absent or `access` (a refresh token can never become a namespace). Missing/garbage/unsafe → `scope: null` and the page then PERSISTS NOTHING (never a shared fallback namespace).
  - `apps/web/src/app/ask/page.tsx` — transcript hydrated from `loadHistory` whenever the account or workspace changes and written back with `saveHistory` on every transcript change; both effects key off the two primitives `[accountScope, workspaceId, storage]`; save is guarded by `transcript.key !== \`${accountScope}::${workspaceId}\`` → return, so a stale transcript can never be written under the new scope's key; visible messages are `transcript.key === scopeKey ? transcript.messages : []` (a switch cannot show the previous workspace's turns); `setSelectedSource(null)` inside the scope effect drops a panel opened in the previous scope; `handleSend` seeds from `current.messages` when already scoped, else re-reads the store for that scope (so a send before hydration cannot overwrite stored history with a 1-message transcript); in-flight flag renamed `streaming → pending`.
  - `apps/web/src/app/ask/components/chat-window.tsx` — prop renamed `streaming → pending`, new exported `PENDING_LABEL = "Waiting for the answer…"`, indicator now `role="status" aria-live="polite"`, doc comment states the actual transport (one request → one response).
  - `apps/web/src/app/ask/components/source-panel.tsx` — new `PassageView` state `deleted` + exported `SOURCE_DELETED_CODE`; `buildPassageUrl(workspaceId, chunkId, documentId?)` / `requestPassage(..., documentId?)` forward the citation's own `document_id` (fetcher keeps its 3rd position — existing callers/tests untouched); `resolvePassage` branches on `source_deleted` BEFORE the answer-embedded fallback, so a deleted source renders the explicit state (red `role="alert"` "Source deleted" + the notice) and, when present, only THIS citation's own recorded text under a "Recorded with this answer (source no longer in the workspace)" label.
  - `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/passage/route.ts` — `PassageParams` gains optional `documentId` (same separator/traversal gate, 400 `invalid_request` on bad input, upstream never contacted); on an upstream chunk **404 with a document ref** the route makes ONE read-only probe of `GET /v1/workspaces/{ws}/documents/{document_id}` (existing, ACL-checked endpoint — the same bearer, same `AbortSignal.any(timeout, request.signal)`): upstream **404 → `source_deleted`**; 200 (still live), 401/403, 5xx, timeout or network failure → unchanged `passage_unavailable`. New `buildDocumentUpstreamUrl` helper.
  - `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/api/passage/route.test.ts` — ONE line: the `readPassageParams` success expectation now includes the added `documentId: null` (assertion strength unchanged; nothing weakened or removed).
  - NEW tests (+51): `ask/history.test.ts` (16), `ask/streaming-claim.test.ts` (5), `ask/page-wiring.test.ts` (7), `ask/deleted-source.test.ts` (6), `(auth)/api/account-scope/route.test.ts` (8), `(auth)/api/passage/deleted-source.test.ts` (9).
  - `handoffs/CODE2-HANDOFF.md` — this row.
WHY: spec instructions 1–2. (1) "Persist history (not React-state-only); scope to account/workspace" — the page previously kept `messages` in `useState` only, so a refresh lost the transcript and a workspace switch showed the previous workspace's turns; the transcript now round-trips through a durable store keyed by BOTH the signed-in account and the active workspace, and the scope is enforced twice (key + envelope) so the metric "cross-workspace leaks: 0" is testable rather than aspirational (a tampered/foreign envelope placed under the right key still restores `[]`). Account identity has no existing surface — verified: `/auth/*` = login/register/refresh/logout, `/v1/me/*` = invitations, no `/me`, and the token is httpOnly — so the account half of the key is read server-side by one small route beside the cookie; it is a NAMESPACE for a browser-local cache and never an authorization input (every read of content still goes to the backend with a verified bearer: R-004). (2) "implement real streaming or remove the streaming claim" — the Ask path is plain request/response (the proxy has no SSE; T-V1-011 captured a single 200 body) and a real streaming implementation would need backend files outside this ticket's allowed list, so the claim was REMOVED: the flag is `pending`, the copy is `Waiting for the answer…`, and `streaming-claim.test.ts` fails on any occurrence of `/stream/i` in either screen — comments included. (3) "Deleted/unavailable passages render an explicit state, never another message's source" — the panel already refused to borrow another message's citation (T-032); what was missing was a way to say *deleted* rather than the vague "unavailable", which the document probe now proves without ever guessing (an outage can never be reported as a deletion), and `deleted` is branched before the embedded-text fallback so the state is visible even when text exists.
REUSED: none (no new dependencies; `package.json` untouched, no new devDependency — which is also why `page-wiring.test.ts` pins the screen's wiring against its source instead of mounting it: vitest runs in the node environment and there is no DOM harness in this repo). Reused existing seams: `next/headers` `cookies()` + `ACCESS_COOKIE` (same pattern as T-V1-032's passage route and the auth routes), the existing ACL-checked document-detail endpoint as the deletion probe, the vitest node config, and the `vi.mock("next/headers")` + stubbed-`fetch` harness from `passage/route.test.ts`.
BUILT: `history.ts` (~330 lines: key/envelope/validation/bounds/failure modes) — no library fits the two hard requirements here (scope-repeated payload that is rejected when it disagrees with its key, and a "never throw, never guess" read on untrusted browser-local bytes); `decodeAccountScope` (~40 lines) — the identity→namespace read that the backend does not expose; `documentIsGone` (~25 lines) — the evidence gate that turns a 404 into a *provable* deletion while keeping every non-404 outcome at `passage_unavailable`.
VERIFY (commands + output):
  - `cd apps/web && npx vitest run` → `Test Files 17 passed (17) / Tests 186 passed (186)` (baseline at `1633d9f` was 11 files / 135 tests → +6 files / +51 tests; zero failures).
  - `cd apps/web && npx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0. `npx eslint src` → exit 0.
  - MUTATION CHECK (`/tmp/opencode/tv1-033/mutation_check.py`, OUTSIDE the repo: each file backed up, mutated, targeted test run, restored and compared byte-identical — `restored byte-identical: True` for all six):
    ```
    M1 cross-workspace/cross-account scope guard removed   -> ask/history.test.ts                 Tests 1 failed | 15 passed (16)
    M2 save becomes a no-op (history no longer persisted) -> ask/history.test.ts                 Tests 7 failed | 9 passed (16)
    M3 panel no longer reports the deleted state          -> ask/deleted-source.test.ts          Tests 3 failed | 3 passed (6)
    M4 route never probes the document                    -> passage/deleted-source.test.ts      Tests 2 failed | 7 passed (9)
    M5 in-flight copy claims incremental delivery again   -> ask/streaming-claim.test.ts         FAIL
    M6 streaming claim reintroduced in the screen         -> ask/streaming-claim.test.ts         Tests 2 failed | 3 passed (5)
    ALL MUTATIONS BIT
    ```
  - CONTROL against the committed T-V1-032 screen (does the suite actually detect the shipped bug? `git show HEAD:apps/web/src/app/ask/page.tsx` written over `page.tsx`, run, then restored — `cmp` byte-identical):
    ```
    Tests  9 failed | 3 passed (12)   (all 7 page-wiring assertions + both page-source claim assertions fail on the old React-only, `streaming`-named page)
    RESTORED_IDENTICAL
    ```
  - The 3 that passed in that control are the two chat-window-only claim assertions and the `PENDING_LABEL` copy — i.e. exactly what the old page did not own.
METRICS: refresh restores: YES (`history.test.ts` round-trip + `page-wiring` hydration assertion; behavior behind the calls covered for real, the React wiring pinned structurally). Cross-workspace leaks: 0 (workspace switch, second workspace, tampered-foreign-envelope-under-the-right-key, and account-on-same-workspace all assert `[]`; `clearHistory` is scope-exact). Cross-account leaks: 0 (different `account` part → different key; unsafe scope → `null` → no write at all). Streaming claim honest: YES (`/stream/i` absent from both screens; `PENDING_LABEL` states only that an answer is pending; indicator announced + composer disabled). Deleted-source tests green: 6 + 9. Transcripts ever written outside their scope: 0 (save guard + envelope check).
REVIEWER CHECKLIST: (1) **no React-only history** — `page.tsx` seeds an EMPTY transcript and fills it from `loadHistory` in the scope effect, writes every change back with `saveHistory`, and `page-wiring.test.ts` + the git-HEAD control prove that reverting to `useState`-only history fails 7/7; (2) **workspace scoping enforced** — key = `account::workspace`, envelope repeats both and is checked on read, save is guarded by transcript-key match, the visible transcript is gated on the live scope, and the open source panel is dropped on switch (6 scope assertions + M1 mutation); (3) **honest streaming claim** — `pending` + `Waiting for the answer…`, no incremental-delivery wording anywhere in either screen, M5/M6 mutations fail the audit.
RISKS: medium-low. (a) History is per-browser Web Storage, not a server-side conversation store: it survives refresh and browser restart but is not synced across devices and is lost if storage is cleared — a durable cross-device store needs a backend table + Alembic migration, outside this ticket's allowed files (disclosed here rather than implied). Size is bounded at 200 turns; a quota failure degrades to in-memory for the session, never to a crash. (b) `decodeAccountScope` reads `sub` WITHOUT verifying the JWT signature — deliberate and documented in-route: it namespaces a browser-local cache only, forging it requires writing an httpOnly cookie for this origin (i.e. already owning the browser profile that holds the localStorage), and it is never consulted for authorization; if the backend ever changes its `sub` format to include a key-unsafe character, the scope comes back `null` and history simply stops persisting (fail-closed, no cross-account fallback). (c) The `source_deleted` verdict needs `document_id` on the citation; citations without it (the backend defaults it to `None`) keep the honest `passage_unavailable` wording — so "deleted" is only ever claimed when provable, at the cost of never being claimed for a ref-less citation. (d) The document probe adds ONE upstream read on the chunk-404 path only (never on success, asserted); it is bounded by the same 10 s deadline and any failure falls back to `passage_unavailable`. (e) No browser/runtime e2e in this ticket (no build, no dev-server restart, no deploy, no commit) — the React wiring is pinned structurally, not rendered; a DOM harness would need a new devDependency. (f) `handoff` evidence for `page-wiring.test.ts` rests on source assertions, which a determined refactor could satisfy while still being wrong — flagged explicitly for review2.

## 2026-10-12 T-V1-038 (wire the router into the Ask path — call-site)
TASK: T-V1-038 — MICRO-TICKETS-V1 "V1 hardening appendix" (S, ~20 min). Trace: V1-COMPLETION-PLAN "router never called", RP-16. Depends T-V1-028 policy = `models.router_config.route()` (TASK-M7-001a, landed). Repo pinned at `8ef9c00`.
CHANGE (3 paths, ALL inside the allowed list — `apps/api/app/ask/router.py` (call-site only) + the handler test + this handoff row; zero other product files):
  - `apps/api/app/ask/router.py` — adds the single per-request call-site: `load_model_config()` builds the three tiers (workspace `routing_rules` seam / permitted `body.model` override / operator default = `ANSWER_MODEL` or `None`), `route()` does the picking, `selection_reason()` labels which tier fired, `resolve_model()` glues the two into `(model, reason)`, and the handler calls it EXACTLY once (`source.count("resolved_model, reason = resolve_model(") == 1` is a test), logs `ask_model_selected`, then `engine.model = resolved_model`. REMOVED from the handler path: `from answer.factory import DEFAULT_ANSWER_MODEL` and the inline `WorkspaceModelConfig(...)/route(...)` block — the file now contains **no model literal and no default-model import** (pinned by a source test banning `gpt-4o-mini` and `DEFAULT_ANSWER_MODEL` in it). Reasons: `policy_veto_local_only` | `permitted_override` | `workspace_rules` | `operator_default` | `operator_default_unconfigured`.
  - `tests/unit/test_ask_query_class.py` — +9 tests (7 → 16): the 3 pinned precedence cases driven through the REAL async handler with a fake engine (configured default / permitted override / unconfigured), plus workspace-tier seam, veto label, override-vs-rules disclosure, no-prompt-content log assertion, and the no-hard-coded-model source pin.
  - `handoffs/CODE2-HANDOFF.md` — this row.
WHY: RP-16 ("router never called") — the policy module existed but the endpoint's model choice needed to be provably single-sourced, ordered by policy, and observable. DECISIONS/M7-001a: precedence stays owned by `models.router_config.route()`; the call site reports, it never decides (reviewer checklist item 3, "no default override"). Structurally, a request bypassing the router is now impossible rather than merely discouraged: there is no second model source on the handler path, and the factory's own default is always overwritten (sentinel assertion).
REUSED: none (no new dependency, no vendored code). Reuses the existing policy `models.router_config.route()`/`WorkspaceModelConfig` as-is and the repo's stdlib `logging` pattern (`knowledge_os.api.*` + `extra={...}` already used by auth/documents).
BUILT: ~60 lines of call-site glue in the allowed file — `(model, reason)` resolution + the tier-label helper. `_rules_selected()` derives the workspace-tier reason by re-asking `route()` with only `routing_rules` stripped, so the logged reason can never disagree with policy precedence (no score logic duplicated). `router_config.py` was NOT touched (not an allowed file here).
VERIFY (exact commands + output):
  - Ticket command: `/root/ko-venv/bin/pytest tests/unit/test_ask_query_class.py tests/unit -q -k "rout or ask"` → **`45 passed, 661 deselected`** (same command on the untouched tree: `36 passed` ⇒ +9, zero regressions in the selected set).
  - `/root/ko-venv/bin/pytest tests/unit/test_ask_query_class.py -q` → **`16 passed`** (baseline 7).
  - Full unit gate: `/root/ko-venv/bin/pytest tests/unit -q` → **`706 passed, 1 warning in 130s`** (the only tests added anywhere are my 9; 706 − 9 = 697 pre-existing).
  - `ruff check` + `ruff format --check` on both files → `All checks passed!` / `2 files already formatted`; `mypy apps/api/app/ask/router.py` → `Success: no issues found in 1 source file`.
  - Repo-wide `ruff check .` → 9 errors, **all pre-existing/foreign and in files I never touched** (`tests/unit/test_ingest_pipeline.py` E501 — another agent's live ingest work; `tests/unit/test_reindex.py` I001+F401; `tests/unit/test_workflow_langflow_adapter.py` ×6). None in `apps/api/app/ask/router.py` or `tests/unit/test_ask_query_class.py`.
  - Mutation controls (edit → run → **byte-identical restore verified with `diff -q`**, artifact `router.py.bak` in `/tmp/opencode/tv1-038/`): (1) drop `engine.model = resolved_model` → **4 failed** (router bypass caught); (2) collapse the override reason to `operator_default` → **2 failed**; (3) reintroduce `preferred_model=os.environ.get("ANSWER_MODEL", "gpt-4o-mini")` → **3 failed** (unconfigured tier + source pin both bite).
METRICS: requests bypassing the router: **0** (one `resolve_model()` call before engine construction + factory-sentinel overwrite asserted in all 3 cases). Precedence cases pinned: **3/3** (configured default → `operator_default` = `operator-model-a`; override → `permitted_override` = `request-override-b`; unconfigured → `operator_default_unconfigured` = policy `FALLBACK_MODEL`). Reason logged per request: **1 event**, 5-value enum, **prompt content in log records: 0** (asserted over every captured record + its extras). Model literals in the handler path: **0**. New/changed test files: 1. Product files outside the allowed list: **0**.
REVIEWER CHECKLIST: (1) **every request routed** — `ask()` reaches `resolve_model()` unconditionally after authz and before `build_answer_engine()`; the fake engine starts at a `FACTORY_SENTINEL` model that every case proves was replaced; (2) **reasons logged sans secrets** — the log line is `event/workspace_id/query_class/model/reason/override_supplied` only, no query text, no `body.model` echo beyond the model name itself, no token/user payload; `test_ask_reason_log_has_no_prompt_content` fails if the query string appears in any record or extra; (3) **no default override** — the handler no longer imports or names any default model (`ANSWER_MODEL` → else `None`, so `models.router_config.FALLBACK_MODEL` decides), and the source test forbids `gpt-4o-mini`/`DEFAULT_ANSWER_MODEL` in the file.
RISKS: medium-low.
  (a) **Precedence wording discrepancy — needs a coordinator ruling.** The ticket words the order as *workspace → permitted override → operator default*, but the landed policy `models.router_config.route()` documents and implements *veto → override → workspace rules → preferred/default* (override BEFORE workspace rules). A call site that re-ordered them would be a second, diverging precedence implementation — exactly what "no default override" forbids — so this ticket defers to `route()` and pins the real order in `test_ask_policy_precedence_keeps_override_above_rules` with the discrepancy written into that test's docstring. If museR wants workspace-above-override, the change belongs in `packages/models/router_config.py` (T-V1-028's file, not allowed here) and this row should be re-opened.
  (b) **The workspace tier has no store yet.** No per-workspace `routing_rules` table or loader exists anywhere in the tree (grep `WorkspaceModelConfig` → only `router_config.py` + tests; micro-tickets-state still has T-V1-028 at QUEUED), so `load_model_config()` returns `routing_rules=()` today. The dependency "T-V1-028 already landed" is true for the **policy** (`route()` + registry) but NOT for a workspace-config loader; the tier is therefore a tested, ordered seam (rules injected in `test_ask_workspace_rules_seam_selects_and_is_labeled` reach `route()` and select `o3-mini`) that is empty in production until that loader lands. Flagged rather than papered over with an invented env var.
  (c) Reason-accuracy limit, disclosed in `_rules_selected()`: if rules were ever to select exactly the default-tier model, the reason records the default tier (selection still correct). Unreachable today because the rules tuple is empty at this call site.
  (d) **Provider identity is not recorded** (that is T-V1-028 instruction 2, not this ticket's) — it would need introspection inside `packages/models/adapters/litellm_provider.py`, outside the allowed files.
  (e) `tests/integration/test_ask_route.py` was **not** executed: it needs live PG/redis and registers users + creates workspaces (writes to the dev DB) — out of scope for a no-deploy/no-restart ticket. Its engine is faked, so the contract it pins is unaffected by this diff; it does not assert on model resolution.
  (f) A stale `apps/api/app/ask/__pycache__/router.cpython-314.pyc` still embeds the old string; it is regenerated on next import and the source pin reads the `.py`.
  (g) No commit / no deploy / no service restart / no `docker compose` command by me; nothing was staged.

  - start (saved `/tmp/opencode/tv1-038/status-start.txt`, HEAD `/tmp/opencode/tv1-038/head-start.txt`): HEAD **`8ef9c00`** (= the ticket pin) + `M handoffs/{COORDINATOR,MUSER-LOOP-STATE,REW1-REVIEW,REW2-REVIEW}-HANDOFF.md` + 12 untracked `handoffs/*.md` (CODE1-Q0, CODE1-Q1, HF-BENCH, HF-BENCHMARK.PARTIAL, HF-BENCHMARK, JEV-ESCALATIONS, PROGRESS-REPORT, REPORTER-HANDOFF, REVIEW1-HANDOFF, REVIEW2-HANDOFF, REW1-FINAL, REW2-V4-B-R1). ## 2026-10-12 T-V1-044 (delete the redundant per-caller zero-vector guard)
TASK: T-V1-044 — MICRO-TICKETS-V1 appendix (microticket, solo, S/~15 min). Trace: T-V1-015 risk note = CODE1-HANDOFF RISKS(1) ("the pipeline keeps its own zero-vector guard; it is now redundant, not incorrect — recommend a follow-up micro-ticket to delete it so checklist item (1) holds literally"). Repo pinned at **`b38aa60`** (= ticket pin; `git rev-parse HEAD` start **and** end both `b38aa60d51aa0d55815aa9a249bc459231b246d2`).
CHANGE (4 paths, 3 allowed + this row — zero product files outside the guard block):
  - `apps/worker/jobs/ingest_pipeline.py` — **+4/−13, guard deletion only**: (1) dropped `_is_zero_vector` from the local `from retrieval.adapters.infinity_embed import ...` line (would otherwise be an unused import); (2) deleted the whole per-caller zero-guard `for position, vec in enumerate(vectors): if not vec or _is_zero_vector(vec): raise ... ZERO_VECTOR_REJECTED ... (retryable=False)` loop; (3) replaced the 4-line TASK-Q1 comment (which documented *that* zero-vector check) with a 3-line comment pointing at the single enforcement point. **Kept untouched:** the `len(vectors) != len(texts)` count check (`EMBED_UNAVAILABLE`, retryable), the `zip(..., strict=True)` record build, the upsert/count-mismatch/`count_ids` verification, every stage/log line.
  - `tests/unit/test_ingest_pipeline.py` — **+30/−0, 2 new tests (9 → 11)**: `test_no_per_caller_zero_vector_guard_in_pipeline` (source pin: `"_is_zero_vector"` and `"ZERO_VECTOR_REJECTED"` must not appear in `jobs/ingest_pipeline.py` — this is the literal **metric "per-caller guards remaining: 0"**) and `test_zero_vector_is_still_rejected_at_the_store_boundary` (the ingest suite itself proves boundary rejection: `validate_record_vector(VectorRecord(vector=[0.0]*4))` → `VectorStoreInvalidVector` with `reason == REJECT_ZERO`).
  - `tests/integration/test_dedup_versions.py` — **+2/−2, comment only**: the `_StubProvider.embed_documents` comment said the fake vectors "satisfy the pipeline's ZERO_VECTOR_REJECTED guard"; now says they satisfy the store boundary (T-V1-015). No code line changed (proved in VERIFY by diffing HEAD vs HEAD+my-comment: **the only two differing lines are those comments**). ⚠️ This ingest test **also went foreign-dirty mid-ticket** (+212 lines from another agent's T-V1-017 stream: `_fts_version_count` helper at +179 and `test_version_replace_exposes_only_new_version_everywhere` at +392) — **not mine, never opened for edit by me**; my delta in the file is exactly the 2 comment lines above.
  - `handoffs/CODE2-HANDOFF.md` — this row.
WHY: T-V1-015's reviewer checklist item (1) is *"single boundary, not per-caller checks"*; with `qdrant_store.validate_record_vector`/`validate_records` now running inside `QdrantVectorStore.upsert` **before the first point is built**, a second check in the ingest caller is dead duplication that keeps the checklist false "literally". DECISIONS/AGENTS rule "one enforcement point": the boundary owns rejection, the pipeline owns job control flow. The guard was also *weaker* than the boundary (it checked only zero/empty, never NaN/Inf/dimension), so deleting it loses no coverage — it hands the case to the check that covers all four classes.
REUSED: none — no new dependency, no vendored code, no adapter change. Reuses the existing boundary (`packages/retrieval/adapters/qdrant_store.py::validate_record_vector`, T-V1-015) and the existing test file's fixtures/marker style.
BUILT: nothing new. The only judgement call — **which lines are "the guard"** — was resolved against the introducing commit: `8599a52` put the `for`-loop zero check at **lines 342-349** exactly (hunk `@@ -264,24 +309,46 @@`, new-file lines 332-349 = 4-line comment, 336-341 = count check, **342-349 = the zero loop**), which is the `~342-349` range the ticket cites. So the loop (+ its comment + its now-unused import) is deleted; the count check at 336-341 is **outside the cited range, is not a zero-vector check, and is kept** (`zip(strict=True)` would otherwise raise a bare `ValueError` instead of a retryable `EMBED_UNAVAILABLE`).
VERIFY (exact commands + output, `TMPDIR=/root/.tmp`):
  - Ticket's boundary + ingest unit command → `/root/ko-venv/bin/pytest -q tests/unit/test_vector_store_boundary.py tests/unit/test_ingest_pipeline.py` → **`25 passed`** (14 boundary + 11 ingest).
  - Ingest unit set → `pytest -q tests/unit/test_vector_store_boundary.py tests/unit/test_ingest_pipeline.py tests/unit/test_ingestion_chunking.py tests/unit/test_ingestion_language.py tests/unit/test_ingestion_parsers.py` → **`49 passed`** (47 before my 2 new tests).
  - Full unit gate → `pytest -q tests/unit` → **`708 passed, 1 warning in 103s`** (706 at HEAD + my 2; **zero regressions anywhere**).
  - Spec ingest+boundary incl. integration → `pytest -q tests/unit tests/integration/test_ingestion_jobs.py tests/integration/test_dedup_versions.py -k "ingest or boundary or dedup"` → **`4 failed, 67 passed, 656 deselected`**.

## 2026-10-12 T-V1-022 (bulk canonical hydration before context build)
TASK: T-V1-022 (micro, M — trace RP-03 · QF-03; depends T-016, landed). Start HEAD **`81b7666`** (the ticket pin) → **`0f36ba2`** mid-run: another agent committed T-V1-017 supersession cleanup while I worked; `git log --oneline 81b7666..HEAD --name-only` shows **no overlap** with `packages/answer/**` or `packages/graph/**`, and all 3 of my modified files were clean at `81b7666`, so my diff is mine only.
CHANGE:
  - `packages/answer/hydration.py` — **NEW (the ONE allowed hydration helper, 298 lines)**: `HydratedChunk` (canonical record: chunk → workspace, document, version-if-exposed, filename, page, section, original text), `CanonicalHydrator` (bulk `hydrate(refs, allowed_workspace_ids, fresh=)` — one deduplicated store pass, scope re-checked on **every** read incl. cache hits, `lookups` counter = the ticket metric, `fresh=True` bypasses+refreshes the cache for the verify/revalidation passes; implements the `ChunkLookup` protocol so the engine can take it unchanged), `hydrate_items()` (context seam), `HydratingOrchestrator` (orchestrator decorator, `__getattr__`-delegating), `allowed_workspace_ids_for()`, `is_routing_hint()`.
  - `packages/answer/factory.py` (**+23/−3**, the `build_answer_engine` block the ticket cites at ≈62–63): builds **one** `CanonicalHydrator(PostgresChunkLookup())` and shares it across both seams — `orchestrator=HydratingOrchestrator(create_orchestrator(), hydrator)` and `chunk_lookup=hydrator if chunk_lookup is not None else None` (no store → `None` → legacy contract, decorator pass-through). `create_orchestrator()` itself is untouched, so the `/search` and `/eval` routes that call it directly are byte-identical to HEAD.
  - `packages/answer/verification.py` (**+178/−90**): `verify_claims` now (1) bulk-hydrates **every** cited ref in one deduplicated `fresh=True` pass **before** any grounding (source lookups on the verify path > 0; in-context non-graph refs used to skip the store entirely — that was the failing control), (2) grounds on **canonical** text and takes provenance from the store record, rejecting any ref the store refuses (missing/deleted/superseded/foreign) *even when a retrieved item carries it*, (3) treats `is_graph`/`routing_hint` items as routing hints that are never a grounding source of their own, and (4) **revalidates lifecycle fresh immediately before returning** (`verify_revalidation_dropped_citations` log), dropping citations and pruning claims left without one. New `_hydrator_for()` + `_filename_of()`; docstrings updated.
  - `packages/graph/retriever.py` (**+17/−3**, `to_retrieved_items` ≈126): every synthesized `[Knowledge Graph Context]` item is tagged `"routing_hint": True` (per-chunk **and** the refless summary-only fallback) and the method docstring states the contract — the description decides *which* refs are candidates, canonical hydration replaces the text before context build, and it is never citable on its own.
  - `tests/unit/test_bulk_hydration.py` — **NEW, 15 tests** (context seam, engine end-to-end, verify seam, reject controls, revalidation, graph-retriever tagging).
  - `handoffs/CODE2-HANDOFF.md` — this row.
WHY: RP-03 / QF-03 — "verification and context see authoritative passages, never payload echoes". Before this ticket the store was consulted only for graph items and for refs *absent* from the retrieved context: an in-context ref (dense/lexical payload or a graph description) was grounded against whatever text retrieval happened to carry, so the jury's 20-vs-900 control passed a hallucinated "900" description as evidence with **0 source lookups on the verify path**, and nothing hydrated the text the model was prompted with. This satisfies the reviewer checklist literally: **(1)** hydration runs at the orchestrator seam, strictly before `prompts.build_prompt`, and again (fresh) before grounding, **(2)** summaries/payload can no longer override the store, **(3)** a fresh lifecycle revalidation is the last thing before the answer returns, so a concurrent delete loses. Satisfies DECISIONS/AGENTS "one enforcement point" (the hydrator is the single canonicalization seam, shared by both call sites) and R-004 (scope is re-checked on every read, including cache hits).
REUSED: none — no new dependency, no vendored code, no adapter change. Reuses the existing `ChunkLookup` seam (`packages/answer/interfaces.py`) and the existing `PostgresChunkLookup`/`MemoryChunkLookup` adapters (46fbf67) **unchanged** — the hydrator composes them, it does not re-implement the SQL, so "current document/version" keeps coming from the adapter's own `document_versions.superseded_at IS NULL` + `documents.deleted_at IS NULL` join. `HydratingOrchestrator` reuses the existing `RetrievalOrchestrator` ABC. No REUSE_MAP/NOTICES delta.
BUILT: the hydrator + decorator themselves (≈300 lines of product glue): no upstream library offers "resolve a candidate ref to the caller-scoped, current-version, original passage across two seams of an answer pipeline", and reuse was actively wrong here — pulling a RAG framework in to replace a 3-way ACL/version/provenance check would move the boundary, not collapse it. Two judgement calls, both documented in code: **the context seam drops what it cannot hydrate** (an item whose ref is missing/deleted/superseded/foreign, or that carries no ref at all) because such an item can never be cited and must never reach the model; and **verify re-reads `fresh`** (rather than trusting the context-pass cache) so verification and the pre-return revalidation observe current lifecycle state.
VERIFY (exact commands + output, from repo root `Super Rag/knowledge-os`):
  - Ticket's test command for the dependent control ticket → `/root/ko-venv/bin/pytest tests/unit/test_answer_engine.py -q` → **`109 passed`** (unchanged suite, zero adaptations — no existing test was touched or weakened).
  - New suite → `/root/ko-venv/bin/pytest tests/unit/test_bulk_hydration.py -q` → **`15 passed`**.
  - Full unit gate → `/root/ko-venv/bin/pytest tests/unit -q` → **`723 passed, 1 warning in 89s`** (708 at my start-of-ticket baseline + my 15; **zero regressions**).
  - Contracts → `/root/ko-venv/bin/pytest tests/contracts -q` → **`97 passed`**.
  - Live services (they are up on this box) → `/root/ko-venv/bin/pytest tests/integration/test_dedup_versions.py::test_version_replace_exposes_only_new_version_everywhere -q` → **`1 passed`** (this is the superseded-v1/live-v2 control that drives the **real** `PostgresChunkLookup` through `verify_claims` — the store-path branch I rewrote, end to end against Postgres); `/root/ko-venv/bin/pytest tests/integration/test_ask_route.py tests/integration/test_security_placeholders.py -q` → **`5 passed, 2 xfailed`** (the 2 xfails are pre-existing placeholders).
  - **Mutation control A (the failing control this ticket exists to fix)** — `canonical = hydrated.get(chunk_id)` → `... if item is None else None` (i.e. in-context refs skip the store, HEAD behaviour) → **7 of my 15 tests fail**, incl. `test_verify_bulk_hydration_reads_the_store_for_in_context_refs`, `test_retrieved_payload_never_overrides_canonical_text`, all 4 `test_reject_controls_*`, `test_lifecycle_revalidated_just_before_returning` and the engine end-to-end. Not vacuous.
  - **Mutation control B** — delete the pre-return revalidation block → **3 fail** (`test_lifecycle_revalidated_just_before_returning`, `test_stable_store_keeps_the_citation`, `test_shared_hydrator_counts_lookups_once_per_request`). **Mutation control C** — make `hydrate_items` a pass-through → **3 fail** (both context-seam tests + the shared-counter test).
  - **Metrics, asserted not asserted-about**: source lookups on the verify path **> 0** (`_CountingLookup.calls > 0` on the exact control where HEAD did 0; `test_shared_hydrator_counts_lookups_once_per_request` pins the per-request arithmetic 1 context + 1 verify + 1 revalidation = **3**); unverified refs cited **0** (every reject control asserts `citations == []` and `verified == []`); duplicate/unhydratable items in context **0** (context-seam test pins `["ch-g", "ch-d"]` exactly).
  - **Live factory probe (real wiring, real Postgres lookup)** — `PYTHONPATH=packages:apps/api:apps/worker:. python -c "...build_answer_engine()..."` → `orchestrator: HydratingOrchestrator / is wrapper: True / inner is real orchestrator: True / delegated .graph: None / delegated .collection: knowledge / chunk_lookup: CanonicalHydrator available: True / shared hydrator: True / grounder: ExtractiveGrounder` — proves the decorator wires, `__getattr__` delegation reaches the inner orchestrator's attributes, and both seams share one hydrator.
  - Lint/type on my paths → `ruff check packages/answer/hydration.py packages/answer/verification.py packages/answer/factory.py packages/graph/retriever.py tests/unit/test_bulk_hydration.py` → **`All checks passed!`**; `ruff format --diff` → **`4 files already formatted`** for factory/verification/hydration/tests; `packages/graph/retriever.py` has exactly **one** format diff and it is the **pre-existing** `summary = (...)` block at `local_search` (verified unformatted at HEAD: `git show HEAD:packages/graph/retriever.py | ruff format --check -` fails there too) — I did **not** reformat someone else's line. `mypy` on my 4 product files → **`Success: no issues found in 4 source files`**.
REVIEWER CHECKLIST: **(1) hydration precedes context AND verify** — `HydratingOrchestrator.retrieve()` hydrates before the engine's `build_prompt` (end-to-end test asserts the *prompt the fake LLM received* contains the canonical "20 dollars" text and contains neither `[Knowledge Graph Context]` nor "900"), and `verify_claims` runs its bulk pass before any `grounder.score`; **(2) summaries uncitable** — retriever tags `routing_hint`, the context seam overwrites the description with canonical text (or drops the item), and verification refuses to ground a hint without canonical text (`test_routing_hint_is_never_a_grounding_source_of_its_own`, `test_no_store_keeps_legacy_contract_but_still_blocks_hints`); **(3) pre-return revalidation present** — the fresh revalidation block is the last await before `return`, killed by mutation control B.
RISKS: low-medium; three need a coordinator note, none blocks review.
  (a) **Fail-closed availability delta (the one real behaviour change on `/ask`).** `PostgresChunkLookup.get` swallows store errors → `None`, so a Postgres outage now empties the context and rejects every citation → `/ask` abstains (`EVIDENCE_MISSING`/`CITATION_INVALID`) instead of answering from unverified payload text. That is the ticket's intent ("never payload echoes", "unverified refs cited: 0") and matches the fail-closed boss ruling, but it IS an availability trade vs HEAD and should be a conscious acceptance, not a surprise.
  (b) **N+1 point reads.** Context pass = one read per unique ref; the verify pass and the revalidation each re-read the surviving citations fresh (a top-10 context with 3 citations ≈ 16 single-row indexed reads per `/ask`). The fix is a bulk `get_many` on `PostgresChunkLookup` — **`packages/answer/adapters/postgres_lookup.py` is not in this ticket's allowlist**, so it is deliberately left as a follow-up microticket rather than smuggled in here.
  (c) **`/search` and `/eval` are intentionally NOT hydrated** — they call `create_orchestrator()` directly (untouched) and are not citation paths. If museR wants the search UI to show canonical text too, that is a one-line route change (`HydratingOrchestrator` + a hydrator) in `apps/api/app/search/search.py`, outside this allowlist.
  (d) **`docs/graph/G4-INTEGRATION-CONTRACT.md` now describes pre-hydration behaviour** (graph summaries become context passages; on the *answer* path they are replaced by canonical chunk text, and a hint that cannot be hydrated is dropped from context entirely). Docs are outside my allowlist — flagging for the coordinator rather than editing.
  (e) **No `document_version_id` on `Citation`** (`packages/answer/models.py` is outside the allowlist), so "current version" is guaranteed by the adapter's own not-superseded/not-deleted join rather than echoed on the citation; `HydratedChunk.document_version_id` is populated only if a future lookup exposes it (the retrieved item's own `document_version_id` is left as-is in that case). A superseded version's chunk cannot hydrate at all, so a stale-version ref is rejected, not mis-versioned.
  (f) **Hydrator cache lifetime** — one instance per `/ask` (the route calls `build_answer_engine()` per request), and every cache hit is re-scoped to the caller's `allowed_workspace_ids`, so no cross-request or cross-workspace reuse is possible even if an engine were ever shared.
  (g) **Concurrent foreign work in the tree (NOT mine, never opened/edited/staged/reverted):** HEAD moved `81b7666 → 0f36ba2` mid-ticket (T-V1-017 commit: `packages/ingestion/dedup.py` + `tests/integration/test_dedup_versions.py` went clean because they were committed); still dirty at my end and foreign — `M packages/retrieval/adapters/qdrant_store.py`, `M handoffs/{COORDINATOR,MUSER-LOOP-STATE,REW1-REVIEW,REW2-REVIEW}-HANDOFF.md`, and the same 12 untracked `handoffs/*.md` reports.
  (h) **No commit / no deploy / no service restart / nothing staged by me.**
GIT SELF-REPORT (`git status --porcelain` in `Super Rag/knowledge-os`, start → end, snapshots in `/tmp/opencode/tv1022/`):

ALL foreign, none mine.
  - end (`status-end.txt`, `head-end.txt` = **`0f36ba2`**, moved by another agent): same foreign set minus `packages/ingestion/dedup.py` / `tests/integration/test_dedup_versions.py` (committed by them), PLUS my **6 paths**: `M packages/answer/verification.py` (+178/−90), `M packages/answer/factory.py` (+23/−3), `M packages/graph/retriever.py` (+17/−3), `?? packages/answer/hydration.py` (298 lines), `?? tests/unit/test_bulk_hydration.py` (524 lines), `M handoffs/CODE2-HANDOFF.md` (this row).

## 2026-10-12 T-V1-025 (ONE shared error taxonomy end-to-end)

TASK: T-V1-025 — "One shared error taxonomy end-to-end" (`tasks/MICRO-TICKETS-V1.md` §E; trace RP-11/QF-11). Repo pin given: `8ec6b96`. Estimate M (≤40 min). No commit / no deploy / no restart / nothing staged.

CHANGE: 9 paths, all inside the allowed set (`packages/core/` taxonomy + API/worker/schemas/TS contract touchpoints + contract test + this row):
- `packages/core/errors.py` — **NEW (~1000 lines), the single shared definition.** `ErrorCategory` (answer/ingestion/indexing/storage/dependency/api/auth — new `dependency` group), `ReasonCode` StrEnum (**57** UPPER_SNAKE codes: 6 answer + 14 ingestion incl. the new `NEEDS_REVIEW` + 3 indexing + 6 store-lifecycle incl. `SUPERSESSION_CLEANUP_FAILED`/`GRAPH_INDEX_PARTIAL` + 6 dependency-availability incl. `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE`/`DEPENDENCY_TIMEOUT`/`RETRIEVAL_UNAVAILABLE`/`RERANK_UNAVAILABLE`/`GRAPH_UNAVAILABLE`/`WORKER_UNAVAILABLE` + 18 legacy-pinned API codes), `ErrorSpec` registry (category, http_status, retryable, safe message, exact `wire` value), `DISCLOSURE_CODES` (codes carried on an otherwise-successful 200/READY result: `NEEDS_REVIEW`, `GRAPH_INDEX_PARTIAL`, `RERANK_UNAVAILABLE`), **`AnswerStatus`** (+ additive `degraded`, `needs_review`) and **`JobStatus`** (+ additive `degraded`, `partial`, `needs_review`), `ANSWER_STATUS_REASON_CODES` / `JOB_STATUS_REASON_CODES` (which code may accompany which status), **required-vs-optional dependency classification per enabled mode** (`ServiceMode` search/ask/ingest/graph × `Dependency` postgres/redis/qdrant/minio/infinity/llm_gateway/graph_store/worker → `DEPENDENCY_MATRIX` + `classify_dependency()` / `required_dependencies()`), and the resolution helpers `canonical_code` / `normalize_code` / `error_detail` / `spec_for` (all fail-open on unknown codes).
- `packages/answer/models.py` — local `AnswerStatus` class **deleted**; re-exported from `core.errors` (one definition, not two). Wire values unchanged.
- `packages/ingestion/persistence.py` — local `JobStatus` class **deleted**; re-exported from `core.errors`. Column stays `String(16)`; **no migration** (all new values ≤ 11 chars).
- `apps/api/app/ask/router.py` — `_http_error` and the 503 `LLMProviderError` envelope now built via `core.errors.error_detail(...)`; envelope shape and wire values byte-identical (`llm_unavailable` is a registered legacy wire value; `cause` passthrough preserved).
- `apps/worker/jobs/ingest_worker.py` — `record_failure` persists `job.error_code = normalize_code(exc.code)` instead of the raw string. **Identity on every code the pipeline emits today** (legacy wire values registered as-is) ⇒ zero stored-value change, one shared code-set at the persistence boundary.
- `apps/web/src/app/ask/contracts.ts` — TS mirror extended: `AnswerStatus` += `degraded`/`needs_review`, **new** `JobStatus`, `AnswerReasonCode` += `DEGRADED_RESULT`, **new** `Dependency`, `ServiceMode`, `DependencyRequirement` unions — all documented as mirrors of `packages/core/errors.py`.

Consequence of extending the union: `tsc --noEmit` refused to compile otherwise. No existing label/class changed; no behavior.
- `tests/contracts/test_error_taxonomy_contract.py` — **NEW, 15 contract tests** (the ticket's "contract test enumerating every code end-to-end"): (1) registry invariants — every code registered, UPPER_SNAKE, safe-message lint (no hostnames/IPs/SQL/tracebacks), `http_status=None` iff ANSWER-category or disclosure, unique wire values, round-trip resolution; (2) **TS mirrors backend** — all 6 TS unions == the Python enums exactly; (3) **no parallel graph taxonomy** — `packages/graph/**` declares no local reason-code class and no raw UPPER_SNAKE `reason_code` literal; (4) **every emitted code is registered** — every `reason_code="…"` literal in `packages/answer/engine.py`, every `IngestPipelineError("…")` literal in `ingest_pipeline.py` (asserted **identity on the wire**, so this ticket moves no value), every typed `IngestionError.code` in `ingestion/normalize.py`, plus greps pinning that the worker persists through `normalize_code` and the ask router builds envelopes through `error_detail`; (5) **required/optional classification** — every dependency classified for every mode, no dep both required and optional in one mode, `/ready`'s probe set fully classified, and Ask/Search mandatory legs (embeddings=infinity, retrieval=qdrant, generation=llm_gateway on ask only) map to REQUIRED deps with registered reason codes.
